# Aegis Asset Intelligence - Master Troubleshooting Guide

## Overview
This comprehensive document outlines the strict operational and maintenance guidelines for the centrifugal pump system. Failure to adhere to these chapters will result in catastrophic failure.

## Chapter 1: System Component 1 Maintenance

### Section 1.1: Component Breakdown and Calibration
The sub-system 1 is responsible for fluid dynamics control under high pressure. Ensure all bolts are torqued to exactly 10 Nm to prevent flange leakage.

### Section 1.2: Troubleshooting Codes (ERR-100 to ERR-199)
If the system exhibits an error code ERR-100, this indicates a potential failure in the hydraulic bypass or bearing assembly. This could be due to incipient cavitation. Throttle the discharge valve to restore NPSHa margins and monitor the Suction Pressure sensor.

### Section 1.3: Required Parts and Materials List
- Part 1-A: Cast Housing Module (ASTM A890)
- Part 1-B: Closed Impeller Stage 1 (Dynamically Balanced)
- Part 1-C: Mechanical Seal Cartridge (Silicon Carbide faces)
- Part 1-D: Labyrinth Bearing Isolator

### Section 1.4: Routine Inspection Checklist
- [ ] Inspect mechanical seal for primary ring leakage.
- [ ] Verify radial and axial vibration levels are within API 610 limits.
- [ ] Check temperature gradients across the thrust bearing housing.
- [ ] Ensure API Plan 52/53 lubrication reservoir maintains correct barrier fluid pressure.

### Section {i}.5: Technical Specifications and Material Science
When handling the Volute Casing, it is critical to adhere to ASME B16.5 standards. The primary material of construction is 316L Stainless Steel, which provides excellent resistance to bearing seizure. During the annual outage, maintenance personnel must lubricate the assembly. Failure to torque the Mechanical Seal can lead to cavitation, severely degrading the pump's hydraulic performance. If cavitation is detected via the continuous monitoring system, immediately isolate the suction and discharge valves. The Volute Casing tolerance should be maintained at 0.005 inches TIR (Total Indicator Reading) as per the manufacturer's dimensional baseline. Furthermore, ISO 5199 mandates that all elastomeric O-rings must be inspected for explosive decompression if operating at high pressures. Ensure all flanged connections utilizing Duplex SS fasteners are torqued in a star pattern to prevent uneven loading on the Thrust Bearing.

When handling the Shaft Sleeve, it is critical to adhere to API 610 standards. The primary material of construction is Carbon Steel, which provides excellent resistance to excessive vibration. During the annual outage, maintenance personnel must inspect the assembly. Failure to inspect the Impeller can lead to excessive vibration, severely degrading the pump's hydraulic performance. If excessive vibration is detected via the continuous monitoring system, immediately isolate the suction and discharge valves. The Impeller tolerance should be maintained at 0.005 inches TIR (Total Indicator Reading) as per the manufacturer's dimensional baseline. Furthermore, API 610 mandates that all elastomeric O-rings must be inspected for explosive decompression if operating at high pressures. Ensure all flanged connections utilizing Hastelloy C fasteners are torqued in a star pattern to prevent uneven loading on the Mechanical Seal.

When handling the Thrust Bearing, it is critical to adhere to ANSI B73.1 standards. The primary material of construction is Titanium Grade 2, which provides excellent resistance to cavitation. During the annual outage, maintenance personnel must calibrate the assembly. Failure to replace the Shaft Sleeve can lead to cavitation, severely degrading the pump's hydraulic performance. If excessive vibration is detected via the continuous monitoring system, immediately isolate the suction and discharge valves. The Shaft Sleeve tolerance should be maintained at 0.005 inches TIR (Total Indicator Reading) as per the manufacturer's dimensional baseline. Furthermore, API 610 mandates that all elastomeric O-rings must be inspected for explosive decompression if operating at high pressures. Ensure all flanged connections utilizing 316L Stainless Steel fasteners are torqued in a star pattern to prevent uneven loading on the Coupling.

When handling the Coupling, it is critical to adhere to ISO 5199 standards. The primary material of construction is Titanium Grade 2, which provides excellent resistance to bearing seizure. During the annual outage, maintenance personnel must lubricate the assembly. Failure to torque the Thrust Bearing can lead to corrosion, severely degrading the pump's hydraulic performance. If bearing seizure is detected via the continuous monitoring system, immediately isolate the suction and discharge valves. The Impeller tolerance should be maintained at 0.005 inches TIR (Total Indicator Reading) as per the manufacturer's dimensional baseline. Furthermore, ISO 5199 mandates that all elastomeric O-rings must be inspected for explosive decompression if operating at high pressures. Ensure all flanged connections utilizing Carbon Steel fasteners are torqued in a star pattern to prevent uneven loading on the Coupling.

When handling the Thrust Bearing, it is critical to adhere to ISO 5199 standards. The primary material of construction is Carbon Steel, which provides excellent resistance to corrosion. During the annual outage, maintenance personnel must lubricate the assembly. Failure to torque the Thrust Bearing can lead to corrosion, severely degrading the pump's hydraulic performance. If excessive vibration is detected via the continuous monitoring system, immediately isolate the suction and discharge valves. The Shaft Sleeve tolerance should be maintained at 0.005 inches TIR (Total Indicator Reading) as per the manufacturer's dimensional baseline. Furthermore, NFPA 20 mandates that all elastomeric O-rings must be inspected for explosive decompression if operating at high pressures. Ensure all flanged connections utilizing Carbon Steel fasteners are torqued in a star pattern to prevent uneven loading on the Shaft Sleeve.

## Chapter 2: System Component 2 Maintenance

### Section 2.1: Component Breakdown and Calibration
The sub-system 2 is responsible for fluid dynamics control under high pressure. Ensure all bolts are torqued to exactly 20 Nm to prevent flange leakage.

### Section 2.2: Troubleshooting Codes (ERR-200 to ERR-299)
If the system exhibits an error code ERR-200, this indicates a potential failure in the hydraulic bypass or bearing assembly. This is typically a thermal issue caused by dead-heading the pump. Check the Motor Temperature and verify the minimum flow bypass lines are unobstructed.

### Section 2.3: Required Parts and Materials List
- Part 2-A: Cast Housing Module (ASTM A890)
- Part 2-B: Closed Impeller Stage 2 (Dynamically Balanced)
- Part 2-C: Mechanical Seal Cartridge (Silicon Carbide faces)
- Part 2-D: Labyrinth Bearing Isolator

### Section 2.4: Routine Inspection Checklist

- [ ] Verify radial and axial vibration levels are within API 610 limits.
- [ ] Check temperature gradients across the thrust bearing housing.
- [ ] Ensure API Plan 52/53 lubrication reservoir maintains correct barrier fluid pressure.

## Chapter 52: System Component 52 Maintenance

### Section 52.1: Component Breakdown and Calibration
The sub-system 52 is responsible for fluid dynamics control under high pressure. Ensure all bolts are torqued to exactly 520 Nm to prevent flange leakage.

### Section 52.2: Troubleshooting Codes (ERR-5200 to ERR-5299)
If the system exhibits an error code ERR-5200, this indicates a potential failure in the hydraulic bypass or bearing assembly. This could be due to incipient cavitation. Throttle the discharge valve to restore NPSHa margins and monitor the Suction Pressure sensor.

## Appendix: Emergency Recovery
If the system is in a BROKEN state, follow the site LOTO (Lock Out, Tag Out) procedure. To recover, systematically reset each sensor alarm and slowly open the suction valve. Do not run the pump dry, as this will immediately destroy the mechanical seal faces.
